- **comment-budget**: Pre-generation comment suppression: doctrine at session start/compact, two-line per-turn reminder, and a deterministic PreToolUse gate that denies changelog/banner comments before they reach disk. ([emmahyde/dotfiles](https://github.com/emmahyde/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-budget))
- **comment-checker**: Detects and warns about unnecessary comments in code changes, encouraging self-documenting code ([swen128/claude-plugins](https://github.com/swen128/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/comment-checker))
- **comment-checker**: PostToolUse hook that flags unnecessary comments. Runs comment-checker, then direnv exec if it is missing. ([systemfsoftware/comment-checker](https://github.com/systemfsoftware/comment-checker))
- **comment-cleaner**: Cleans up the comments already in a codebase - cuts the ones that only restate the code, shortens verbose agent-written ones, and keeps the ones carrying context you cannot get back. Comments only: every edit is checked against a comment-free skeleton of the file, and a ledger lets a large repository be worked through over several sessions without missing a file or doing one twice. ([wrxck/comment-cleaner](https://github.com/wrxck/comment-cleaner))
- **comment-cleanup**: Aggressively prune comments from source code so every remaining comment earns its place. Nudges after Edit/Write and gates turn-end until pending files are cleaned. ([emmahyde/dotfiles](https://github.com/emmahyde/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-cleanup))
- **comment-conventions**: Language-agnostic code commenting and docblock conventions — present-tense comments, dev-focused content, mandatory docblocks on new functions/methods/components, a 3-source-line descending-length cap on docblock prose, and a no-chatter rule for inline comments (no prose walls, no business-logic essays, no step-by-step narration in the code path). Auto-fixes non-compliant docblocks and inline-comment walls on existing functions in the file being edited, leaving unusually long deliberate docblocks alone. Includes a docblock validator script for checking Rule 4 on demand. ([rvanbaalen/skills](https://github.com/rvanbaalen/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-conventions))
- **comment-cut**: Cut comment bloat out of a branch without losing the comments that matter — four keep-rules, each needing a failure that nothing reports AND a tempting edit that would cause it, because a visible bug is a missing test rather than a comment; specs default to zero on the principle that a red test is not a silent failure. Reachable as /comment-cut:cut, and a PreToolUse gate stops a commit or a PR once when the change adds bloat on the lines it touches — opt-in per repo, fail-open on every path, and the retry always proceeds ([shawnroos/shrimpshack](https://github.com/shawnroos/shrimpshack/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-cut))
- **comment-discipline**: Blocks comments that only make sense next to the diff. ([JimCline/claudetools](https://github.com/JimCline/claudetools/tree/HEAD/comment-discipline))
- **comment-discipline**: Rules for writing source-code comments that serve future readers, not the conversation that produced the code. Prevents the common AI-coding failure mode where agents leak chat context into code comments. ([mikitahimpel/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/mikitahimpel/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-discipline))
- **comment-gardener**: Standards-aware maintenance for comments and doc comments, from stale-only repair to concise zen pruning. ([msmorgan/comment-gardener](https://github.com/msmorgan/comment-gardener))
- **comment-haijin**: Blocks new prose comments that don't fit 5-7-5 haiku form inside /** ... */ blocks. ([bagelbits/claude-skills](https://github.com/bagelbits/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-haijin))
- **comment-hygiene**: SessionStart hook that injects code-comment discipline into every session — every comment must earn its place: say what the code cannot, never duplicate it; bans narration, changelog comments, reviewer asides, signature-restating docstrings, and leaky provenance ([lexfrei/ccc](https://github.com/lexfrei/ccc/tree/HEAD/hooks/comment-hygiene))
- **comment-hygiene**: Enforce consistent comment style as the agent writes code — one-line comments stay one line, template/HTML comments never leak into the DOM, every method/class you write gets a doc comment (JSDoc, Javadoc, C# XML doc, docstring, godoc, rustdoc, YARD, PHPDoc, GDScript ##) with the right params/return/purpose, and every unit test is marked // Arrange, // Act, // Assert — never backfilling docs on existing code unless asked. ([mkolew/ai](https://github.com/mkolew/ai/tree/HEAD/skills/comment-hygiene))
- **comment-in-the-hat**: Blocks new prose comments that don't pair into rhyming anapestic couplets (AABB). ([bagelbits/claude-skills](https://github.com/bagelbits/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-in-the-hat))

([shipshitdev/skills](https://github.com/shipshitdev/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/comment-mode))

- **comment-review**: Browser-preview comment loop for markdown/HTML artifacts — right-click an element to anchor a comment by CSS selector, apply the queue back as edits, hot-reload ([jongwony/cc-plugin](https://github.com/jongwony/cc-plugin/tree/HEAD/comment-review))
- **comment-reviewer**: Reviews code comments across languages before a PR opens. Use when finishing a development branch, before merging, or... ([kettleofketchup/KettleOfSkills](https://github.com/kettleofketchup/KettleOfSkills/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-reviewer))
- **comment-rot**: Detects comment rot — comments that no longer match the code they describe — with layered semantic analysis instead of regex. PostToolUse hook + /comment-rot:scan and :fix commands. ([Bouwles/comment-rot-detector](https://github.com/Bouwles/comment-rot-detector))
- **comment-rule**: Ships a UserPromptSubmit hook that prepends 'Remember the code commenting rule.' to every prompt, plus a skill containing the rule itself (when to comment, when not to). ([amims71/claude-plugins](https://github.com/amims71/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-rule))
- **comment-slop**: Find and remove AI-slop comments — ones that restate the code, describe another layer's behavior, or narrate planning that never shipped — while keeping the comments that carry real reasoning. Comment the why, not the what or how: names carry the what, and delete is the default verdict. Use when a reviewer calls a comment useless or AI-written, when auditing comments and docstrings on a branch or PR, when deciding whether new code needs a comment, or before sending a change for review. ([dashed/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/dashed/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/comment-slop))
